\begin{abstract}Every move below was produced by relational analogy to named master games (human and/or top-engine -- the exact source is named per move). Each figure shows our position with Melanie's move (a) and the top retrieved analog positions (b--d) with the master move played there; the caption names the exact source and the piece-role correspondence under $\mu$ -- cross-type maps (a rook playing a queen's role) are called out, as no material/eval engine explains a move that way.\end{abstract}

\caption{\textbf{Move 7, Black: \texttt{e6}.} (a) Our position; Melanie plays \texttt{e6} (\texttt{e7e6}). Panels (b)--(d) are the retrieved master positions whose correspondence most drove this move (highest QAP objective among those that transfer to it). (b) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#1217} (ply 13) [structural distance $d$=34, QAP obj=89]: the master played \texttt{a5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pe2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pa2}; cross-type roles: our \emph{R}a8 as their \emph{N}b8, our \emph{N}b8 as their \emph{P}b7; transferred move \texttt{e7e6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (c) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0002\#1218} (ply 13) [structural distance $d$=34, QAP obj=89]: the master played \texttt{a5}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pe2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Pa2}; cross-type roles: our \emph{R}a8 as their \emph{N}b8, our \emph{N}b8 as their \emph{P}b7; transferred move \texttt{e7e6} \textbf{(endorses this move)}. (d) Retrieved from \texttt{ccrl\_top\_p0000\#730} (ply 13) [structural distance $d$=48, QAP obj=51]: the master played \texttt{Nbd7}; under $\mu$ our \emph{Pc2} plays the role of that game's \emph{Nb1} (a \textbf{cross-type} correspondence); cross-type roles: our \emph{p}d4 as their \emph{R}a8, our \emph{P}e7 as their \emph{B}f8; transferred move \texttt{c7c6}. Synthesis: 2 retrieved analogs transferred to \texttt{e6} (summed QAP objective 178, the strongest correspondence among the candidate moves); Melanie committed it via the \texttt{search} selector.}
\label{fig:thought-m13}
